\displaystyle\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}|\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{t}\rangle x_{i}^{\perp}\right\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\left\|\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}-\mathbb{E}_{\{x_{i}\}_{i\in[n]}\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})}\right\}|\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{t}\rangle\tilde{x}_{i}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2\sqrt{\frac{(d-2)}{n}}+2\frac{\log\frac{2}{\delta}}{n}+4\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2}{\delta}}{n}}\right)+\max\left(20\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{8}{\delta}}{n}},45\frac{\log\frac{8}{\delta}}{n}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)

Bound for EM

For the standard EM, We update parameters
\theta
with

\begin{array}[]{lll}\theta^{t+1}\leftarrow M_{n}(\theta^{t},\nu^{t})&=&\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]^{-1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}\tanh\left(\frac{y_{i}\langle x_{i},\theta^{t}\rangle}{\sigma^{2}}+\nu^{t}\right)y_{i}x_{i}\end{array}

In noiseless setting (
\sigma\rightarrow 0
, SNR
\rightarrow\infty
),
consider the difference between EM updates at the finite-sample/population level.

\displaystyle M_{n}(\theta^{t},\nu^{t})-M(\theta^{t},\nu^{t})

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]^{-1}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim p(s\mid\theta^{\ast},\pi^{\ast})}\right\}|\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{t}\rangle x_{i}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]^{-1}\left\{I_{d}-\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]\right\}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim p(s\mid\theta^{\ast},\pi^{\ast})}|\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{t}\rangle x_{i}

By using
[
42
]

page 162,
equation (6.9), where
\gamma_{\min}
is the minimum eigen value.

\mathbb{P}\left[\sqrt{\gamma_{\min}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]}\leq(1-\delta)-\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\right]\leq\mathrm{e}^{-n\delta^{2}/2}

Let
\mathrm{e}^{-n\delta^{2}/2}\leftarrow\delta,\delta\leftarrow\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}
, with probability at least
1-\delta

\sqrt{\gamma_{\min}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]}\geq 1-\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}-\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}=\mathcal{O}(1)

Using
\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim p(s\mid\theta^{\ast},\pi^{\ast})}|\langle x,\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle x,\theta^{t}\rangle x=\mathrm{sgn}(\rho)\left[1-\frac{\arccos|\rho|}{\frac{\pi}{2}}\right]\frac{\theta^{\ast}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}+\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)^{-1}\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\frac{\theta^{t}}{\|\theta^{t}\|}

Hence
\frac{\|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim p(s\mid\theta^{\ast},\pi^{\ast})}|\langle x,\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle x,\theta^{t}\rangle x\|_{2}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}=\sqrt{\left[1-\frac{\arccos|\rho|}{\frac{\pi}{2}}\right]^{2}+\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)^{-2}(1-\rho^{2})+\frac{4}{\pi}|\rho|\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\left[1-\frac{\arccos|\rho|}{\frac{\pi}{2}}\right]}\in[0,1]

By using
[
42
]

page 162,
equation (6.12)

\mathbb{P}\left[\left\|\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]-I_{d}\right\|_{2}\geq 2\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}+2\delta+\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}+\delta\right)^{2}\right]\leq 2\mathrm{e}^{-n\delta^{2}/2}

let
2\mathrm{e}^{-n\delta^{2}/2}\leftarrow\delta,\delta\leftarrow\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2}{\delta}}{n}}
, with probability at least
1-\delta

\left\|\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]-I_{d}\right\|_{2}\leq 2\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}+2\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2}{\delta}}{n}}\right)+\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}+\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2}{\delta}}{n}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\frac{\|M_{n}(\theta^{t},\nu^{t})-M(\theta^{t},\nu^{t})\|_{2}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\gamma_{\min}\left[\frac{\sum_{i\in[n]}}{n}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]\right\}^{-1}\cdot\left\{\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\left\|\left\{\frac{\sum_{i\in[n]}}{n}-\mathbb{E}\right\}|\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{t}\rangle x_{i}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\left[\frac{\sum_{i\in[n]}}{n}x_{i}x_{i}^{\top}\right]-I_{d}\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)+\left\{2\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}+2\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2}{\delta}}{n}}\right)+\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}+\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2}{\delta}}{n}}\right)^{2}\right\}}{\mathcal{O}(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)

∎

E.3
.

(Convergence of
\theta^{t}
in Single Iteration)
For
\vartheta:=\sin\varphi\hat{e}_{1}+\cos\varphi\hat{e}_{2}
,
where
\{\hat{e}_{1},\hat{e}_{2}\}
is an orthonormal basis for the subspace

\operatorname{span}\{\hat{e}_{1},\hat{e}_{2}\}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and
\varphi\in\left(0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right)
; with a pertubation vector
\varrho\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
with lengh
\|\varrho\|=r\in(0,\sin\varphi)
; then the
angle
\varphi^{\prime}:=\arcsin\frac{|\langle\vartheta+\varrho,\hat{e}_{1}\rangle|}{\|\vartheta+\varrho\|}
, satisfies
\varphi^{\prime}\geq\varphi-\arcsin r

Proof.

Note that with
\|\varrho\|=r\in(0,\sin\varphi)
,
\langle\vartheta+\varrho,\hat{e}_{1}\rangle=\langle\vartheta,\hat{e}_{1}\rangle+\langle\varrho,\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\geq\sin\varphi-\|\varrho\|=\sin\varphi-r>0
, thus

\sin\varphi^{\prime}=\frac{\langle\vartheta+\varrho,\hat{e}_{1}\rangle}{\|\vartheta+\varrho\|}>0

Express the pertubation vector by
\varrho=-r^{\prime}\cos(\varphi-\Delta)\hat{e}_{1}+r^{\prime}\sin(\varphi-\Delta)\hat{e}_{2}+\sqrt{r^{2}-\left[{r^{\prime}}\right]^{2}}\hat{e}
, where
r^{\prime}\in[0,r],\Delta\in(-\pi,\pi]
and

\hat{e}\in\operatorname{span}\{\hat{e}_{1},\hat{e}_{2}\}^{\perp},\|\hat{e}\|=1

\displaystyle\langle\vartheta+\varrho,\hat{e}_{1}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\vartheta,\hat{e}_{1}\rangle+\langle\varrho,\hat{e}_{1}\rangle=\sin\varphi-r^{\prime}\cos(\varphi-\Delta)

\displaystyle\|\vartheta+\varrho\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|[\sin\varphi-r^{\prime}\cos(\varphi-\Delta)]\hat{e}_{1}+[\cos\varphi+r^{\prime}\sin(\varphi-\Delta)]\hat{e}_{2}+\sqrt{r^{2}-\left[{r^{\prime}}\right]^{2}}\hat{e}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{[\sin\varphi-r^{\prime}\cos(\varphi-\Delta)]^{2}+[\cos\varphi+r^{\prime}\sin(\varphi-\Delta)]+\left[r^{2}-\left[{r^{\prime}}\right]^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{[1+r^{2}]-2r^{\prime}\sin\Delta}

Hence, let
p:=\frac{r^{\prime}|\sin\Delta|}{r},-r^{\prime}\cos\Delta\geq-\sqrt{[r^{\prime}]^{2}-[rp]^{2}}\geq-\sqrt{r^{2}-[rp]^{2}}=-r\sqrt{1-p^{2}}

and
p=\frac{r^{\prime}}{r}|\sin\Delta|\leq 1

\displaystyle\sin\varphi^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\sin\varphi-r^{\prime}\cos(\varphi-\Delta)}{\sqrt{[1+r^{2}]-2r^{\prime}\sin\Delta}}r

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{[1-r^{\prime}\sin\Delta]\sin\varphi-r^{\prime}\cos\Delta\cos\varphi}{\sqrt{[1+r^{2}]-2r^{\prime}\sin\Delta}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{[1-rp]\sin\varphi-r\sqrt{1-p^{2}}\cos\varphi}{\sqrt{[1+r^{2}]-2rp}}:=\psi(p)

For
\psi(p):=\frac{[1-rp]\sin\varphi-r\sqrt{1-p^{2}}\cos\varphi}{\sqrt{[1+r^{2}]-2rp}}
for
p\in[0,1]
, note that
\cos\left(\varphi+\left[\frac{\pi}{2}-\arcsin p\right]\right)\leq\cos\varphi

Thus
\cos\varphi-r\cos\left(\varphi+\left[\frac{\pi}{2}-\arcsin p\right]\right)>0

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}p}\log\psi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{-r\sin\varphi+r\frac{p}{\sqrt{1-p^{2}}}\cos\varphi}{[1-rp]\sin\varphi-r\sqrt{1-p^{2}}\cos\varphi}-\frac{1}{2}\cdot\frac{-2r}{[1+r^{2}]-2rp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{r\cdot\left\{\cos\varphi-r\left[p\cos\varphi-\sqrt{1-p^{2}}\sin\varphi\right]\right\}(p-r)}{\sqrt{1-p^{2}}\left\{[1-rp]\sin\varphi-r\sqrt{1-p^{2}}\cos\varphi\right\}\cdot\{[1+r^{2}]-2rp\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{r\cdot\left\{\cos\varphi-r\cos\left(\varphi+\left[\frac{\pi}{2}-\arcsin p\right]\right)\right\}(p-r)}{\sqrt{1-p^{2}}\left\{[1-rp]\sin\varphi-r\sqrt{1-p^{2}}\cos\varphi\right\}\cdot\{[1+r^{2}]-2rp\}}

therefore,
\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}p}\log\psi<0,\forall p\in(0,r);\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}p}\log\psi>0,\forall p\in(r,1)
, hence
\psi(p)\geq\psi(p)\mid_{p=r}
for
p\in[0,1]

\displaystyle\sin\varphi^{\prime}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\psi(p)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\psi(p)\mid_{p=r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-r^{2}}\sin\varphi-r\cos\varphi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sin(\varphi-\arcsin r)

Note that
r\in(0,\sin\varphi)
, that is
\frac{\pi}{2}>\varphi>\varphi-\arcsin r>0
, and we show that

\varphi^{\prime}\geq\varphi-\arcsin r

∎

E.2
Initialization and Convergence of
\theta

E.4
.

(Proposition
5.4
in Section
5
: Initialization with Easy EM)
In the noiseless setting, suppose we run the sample-splitting finite-sample
Easy EM with
n^{\prime}:=\Theta\left(\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\wedge\left[\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\right]^{2}\right)
fresh
samples for each iteration, then after at most
T_{0}=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)
iterations, it satisfies
\varphi^{T_{0}}\geq\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)
with probability at least
1-\delta
.

Proof.

Suppose we run finite-sample easy EM with refresh samples
n^{\prime}
for each iterations, then after some iterations
\varphi\geq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}
.
We will prove this in the followings. Let’s denote
\hat{\theta},\hat{\varphi}
the EM update at population level.
With the EM update
\hat{\theta}^{t+1}:=M(\theta^{t})
and let
\tilde{x}_{i}=\lambda_{1i}\vec{e}_{1}+\lambda_{2i}\vec{e}_{2}
then
\theta^{\ast}=\rho\vec{e}_{1}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\vec{e}_{2}
.
We begin with evaluating or estimating some expectations of Gaussian.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\lambda_{1i},\lambda_{2i}\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\left|\rho\lambda_{1i}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\lambda_{2i}\right|\operatorname{sgn}(\lambda_{1i})\lambda_{2i}=\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)^{-1}\operatorname{sgn}(\rho)\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\left[\frac{\pi}{2}-\arccos|\rho|\right]\in[-0.357205,0.357205]

The 2nd moment is
\mathbb{E}_{\lambda_{1i},\lambda_{2i}\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\left|\rho\lambda_{1i}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\lambda_{2i}\right|^{2}\operatorname{sgn}(\lambda_{1i})^{2}\lambda^{2}_{2i}=3-2\rho^{2}
. The 3rd moment is bounded by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\lambda_{1i},\lambda_{2i}\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\left\{\left|\left|\rho\lambda_{1i}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\lambda_{2i}\right|\operatorname{sgn}(\lambda_{1i})\lambda_{2i}\right|^{3}\right\}\leq\left[\mathbb{E}_{\lambda_{1i},\lambda_{2i}\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\left|\rho\lambda_{1i}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\lambda_{2i}\right|^{4}|\lambda_{2i}|^{4}\right]^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[105-120\rho^{2}+24\rho^{4}]^{\frac{3}{4}}\leq 105^{\frac{3}{4}}

Let
X:=\left|\rho\lambda_{1i}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\lambda_{2i}\right|\operatorname{sgn}(\lambda_{1i})\lambda_{2i}
, thus
3\geqslant\operatorname{Var}[X]\geq 3-2\rho^{2}-0.357205^{2}\geq 2.8724-2\rho^{2}

By Minkovski inequality,
\mathbb{E}[|X-\mathbb{E}[X]|^{3}]\leq(\mathbb{E}[|X|^{3}]^{\frac{1}{3}}+|\mathbb{E}[X]|)^{3}\leq\left(\left[105^{\frac{3}{4}}\right]^{\frac{1}{3}}+0.357205\right)^{3}\leq 45.054
.

Then, let’s decompose the statistical error into three terms.

\displaystyle\theta^{t+1}-\hat{\theta}^{t+1}=M^{\operatorname{easy}}_{n}(\theta^{t})-M(\theta^{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in[n^{\prime}]}|\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{t}\rangle x_{i}^{\perp}+\|\theta^{\ast}\|\underset{T_{2}}{\underbrace{\left[\left\{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in[n^{\prime}]}-\mathbb{E}_{\lambda_{1i},\lambda_{2i}\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\right\}\left|\rho\lambda_{1i}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\lambda_{2i}\right|\operatorname{sgn}(\lambda_{1i})\lambda_{2i}\right]}}\vec{e}_{2}

\displaystyle+\|\theta^{\ast}\|\underset{T_{1}}{\underbrace{\left[\left\{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in[n^{\prime}]}-\mathbb{E}_{\lambda_{1i},\lambda_{2i}\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\right\}\left|\rho\lambda_{1i}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\lambda_{2i}\right||\lambda_{1i}|\right]}}\vec{e}_{1}

Consider such events
\mathcal{E}_{1}:=\left\{|T_{1}|\leq\frac{2}{\pi}\right\},\mathcal{E}_{2}:=\left\{|T_{2}|\geq c\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\right\}
.
Note that the variance of
T_{1}

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}[T_{1}]=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\operatorname{Var}\left[\left|\rho\lambda_{1i}+\sqrt{1-\rho^{2}}\lambda_{2i}\right||\lambda_{1i}|\right]\leq\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}[3-1^{2}]=\frac{2}{n^{\prime}}.

Hence,
\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}]=1-\mathbb{P}\left\{|T_{1}|\geq\frac{2}{\pi}\right\}\geq 1-2\frac{\operatorname{Var}[T_{1}]}{\left(\frac{2}{\pi}\right)^{2}}\geq 1-2\frac{\frac{2}{n^{\prime}}}{\left(\frac{2}{\pi}\right)^{2}}=1-\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}
.

Consider
\mathcal{E}_{2}
, with
Berry-Esseen
bound for the central limit
theorem,
[
37
]
theorem 1.1, where
T_{2}=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in[n^{\prime}]}.[X_{i}-\mathbb{E}[X_{i}]]

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}T_{2}}{\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}[X]}}\leq\frac{c}{\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}[X]}}\right]-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}[X]}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{0.4785}{(\operatorname{Var}[X])^{\frac{3}{2}}}\mathbb{E}[|X-\mathbb{E}[X]|^{3}]

Thus, with
3\geqslant\operatorname{Var}[X]\geq 3-2\rho^{2}-0.357205^{2}\geq 2.8724-2\rho^{2}=2.8724-\sin^{2}\varphi^{t}\geq 2.8724-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}
,

and
\mathbb{E}[|X-\mathbb{E}[X]|^{3}]\leq 45.054
,
2.8724-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\geq 2.8724-\frac{1}{3}\geq 2.539
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[T_{2}\geq c\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}T_{2}}{\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}[X]}}\leq\frac{c}{\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}[X]}}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}[X]}}\right)-\frac{0.4785}{(\operatorname{Var}[X])^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{\mathbb{E}[|X-\mathbb{E}[X]|^{3}]}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)-\frac{0.4785}{\left(2.8724-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot\frac{45.054}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\geqslant

\displaystyle 1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)-\left(\frac{0.4785\cdot 45.054}{2.539^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)-5.3287\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}

\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{2}]=\mathbb{P}\left[T_{2}\geq c\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\right]+\mathbb{P}\left[-T_{2}\geq c\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\right]\geq 2\left[1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right]-2\cdot 5.3287\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}\wedge\mathcal{E}_{2}]=\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}]+\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{2}]-\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}\vee\mathcal{E}_{2}]\geq\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}]+\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{2}]-1

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}\right)+\left(2\left[1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right]-2\cdot 5.3287\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right)-1\geq 2\left[1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right]-\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{11}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right]

After runing Easy-EM for
\mathcal{T}
times with independent batches (batch size is

n^{\prime}
)

To ensure that the probability of
\overline{\mathcal{E}_{1}\wedge\mathcal{E}_{2}}
happens for

\mathcal{T}
times is less than
\delta
, and let
\mathcal{T}=\Theta\left(\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)
and
\left\{2\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)-1+\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{11}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right]\right\}<1
for large enough
n^{\prime}>\left\{\frac{11+\sqrt{11^{2}+8\pi^{2}\left[1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right]}}{4\left[1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right]}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle(1-\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}\wedge\mathcal{E}_{2}])^{\mathcal{T}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{1-2\left[1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right]+\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{11}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right]\right\}^{\mathcal{T}}=\left\{2\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)-1+\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{11}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right]\right\}^{\mathcal{T}}\leq\delta

Otherwise, if
\mathcal{E}_{1}\wedge\mathcal{E}_{2}=\left\{|T_{1}|\leq\frac{2}{\pi}\right\}\wedge\left\{|T_{2}|\geq c\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\right\}
happens in the
\mathcal{T}
-th
iteration

\displaystyle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{\theta}^{\mathcal{T}+1}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in[n^{\prime}]}|\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\mathcal{T}}\rangle x_{i}^{\perp}+\|\theta^{\ast}\|[T_{1}\vec{e}_{1}+T_{2}\vec{e}_{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in[n^{\prime}]}|\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\mathcal{T}}\rangle x_{i}^{\perp}+\|\theta^{\ast}\|[T_{1}\vec{e}_{1}+T_{2}\vec{e}_{2}]

Then, since
\left\|\frac{M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\right\|\leq 1
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\left\|\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in[n^{\prime}]}|\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\mathcal{T}}\rangle x_{i}^{\perp}\right\|\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{d-2}{n^{\prime}}}\left[1+\frac{1}{(d-2)-1}\left(2\log\frac{1}{\delta}+1.62\right)\right]=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n^{\prime}}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)

If we assume
2\sqrt{\frac{d-2}{n^{\prime}}}\left[1+\frac{1}{(d-2)-1}\left(2\log\frac{1}{\delta}+1.62\right)\right]\leq 0.1
for large
n^{\prime}

Thus, we conclude that
n^{\prime}\geq\frac{8\cdot 2.62^{2}}{0.1^{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{\|\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1}\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\left\|\frac{M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}+T_{1}\vec{e}_{1}+T_{2}\vec{e}_{2}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\left\|\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\sum_{i\in[n^{\prime}]}|\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\operatorname{sgn}\langle\tilde{x}_{i},\theta^{\mathcal{T}}\rangle x_{i}^{\perp}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}+T_{1}\vec{e}_{1}+T_{2}\vec{e}_{2}\right\|+0.1\leq\left(1+\frac{2}{\pi}+|T_{2}|\right)+0.1=\left(1.1+\frac{2}{\pi}\right)+|T_{2}|

Using the results,
\left\langle\frac{M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})-\theta^{\mathcal{T}}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|},\vec{e}_{2}\right\rangle=\frac{2}{\pi}\varphi^{\mathcal{T}}\cos(\varphi^{\mathcal{T}}),\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}},\vec{e}_{2}\rangle=0
and

\frac{\pi}{2}\leq\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}},\vec{e}_{1}\rangle=\frac{\theta^{\mathcal{T}}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\leq 1
and
|T_{1}|\leq\frac{2}{\pi}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\vec{e}_{1}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\frac{M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})-\theta^{\mathcal{T}}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|},\vec{e}_{1}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}},\vec{e}_{1}\rangle+T_{1}\right|\leq\left|T_{1}+\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}},\vec{e}_{1}\rangle\right|+\left|\left\langle\frac{M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})-\theta^{\mathcal{T}}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|},\vec{e}_{1}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{2}{\pi}+1\right)+\frac{\|M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})-\theta^{\mathcal{T}}\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\leq\left(\frac{2}{\pi}+1\right)+\left(1-\frac{2}{\pi}\right)=2

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\vec{e}_{2}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\frac{M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})-\theta^{\mathcal{T}}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|},\vec{e}_{2}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}},\vec{e}_{2}\rangle+T_{2}\right|\geq|T_{2}|-\left\langle\frac{M(\theta^{\mathcal{T}})-\theta^{\mathcal{T}}}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|},\vec{e}_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle|T_{2}|-\frac{2}{\pi}\varphi^{\mathcal{T}}\cos(\varphi^{\mathcal{T}})\geq|T_{2}|-\frac{2}{\pi}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}

Then, with
n^{\prime}\geq\frac{8\cdot 2.62^{2}}{0.1^{2}}
, we conclude that

\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\leq\frac{0.1}{2\sqrt{2}\cdot 2.62}\leq 0.0135,\sqrt{1-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\geq\sqrt{1-\frac{0.1^{2}}{8\cdot 2.62^{2}}}\geq 1-0.1^{4}

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|^{2}}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\right|=\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\right|=\left|\sin\varphi^{t}\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\vec{e}_{1}\rangle+\cos\varphi^{t}\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\vec{e}_{2}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\cos\varphi^{t}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\vec{e}_{2}\rangle\right|-\sin\varphi^{t}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\vec{e}_{1}\rangle\right|\geq\sqrt{1-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\cdot\left[|T_{2}|-\frac{2}{\pi}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\right]-\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\cdot 2

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left[(1-0.1^{4})\left(\frac{|T_{2}|}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}}-\frac{2}{\pi}\right)-2\right]\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}

and
|\rho|=\sin\varphi^{\mathcal{T}}\leq\varphi^{\mathcal{T}}<\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}
,
with
\mathcal{E}_{1}\wedge\mathcal{E}_{2}=\left\{|T_{1}|\leq\frac{2}{\pi}\right\}\wedge\left\{|T_{2}|\geq c\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\right\}
.

By solving
\frac{\left[(1-0.1^{4})\left(c-\frac{2}{\pi}\right)-2\right]}{\left(1.1+\frac{2}{\pi}\right)+0.0135c}\geq 1
, we obtain
c\geq 4.43347
, then

\displaystyle\varphi^{\mathcal{T}+1}\geq\sin\varphi^{\mathcal{T}+1}=\frac{|\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|}{\|\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1}\|\cdot\|\theta^{\ast}\|}=\frac{\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|^{2}}\langle\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\right|}{\frac{\|\theta^{\mathcal{T}+1}\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}}\geq\frac{\left[(1-0.1^{4})\left(\frac{|T_{2}|}{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}}-\frac{2}{\pi}\right)-2\right]\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}}{\left(1.1+\frac{2}{\pi}\right)+|T_{2}|}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\left[(1-0.1^{4})\left(c-\frac{2}{\pi}\right)-2\right]}{\left(1.1+\frac{2}{\pi}\right)+c\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\geq\frac{\left[(1-0.1^{4})\left(c-\frac{2}{\pi}\right)-2\right]}{\left(1.1+\frac{2}{\pi}\right)+0.0135c}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}\geq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}

By choosing
c=4.43347
, for large
n^{\prime}\geq 1.103582\times 10^{6}
, we have

(1-0.01048)+\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{11}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right]<1

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}\wedge\mathcal{E}_{2}]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 2\left[1-\Phi\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{3}}\right)\right]-\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{11}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right]=0.01048-\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{11}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right]

\displaystyle 1-\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}\wedge\mathcal{E}_{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-0.01048)+\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{11}{\sqrt{n^{\prime}}}\right]<1

With at most
\mathcal{T}=\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{-\log(1-\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}_{1}\wedge\mathcal{E}_{2}])}=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)
iterations,
we can ensure
\varphi^{T_{0}}>\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}
for some
T_{0}\in[\mathcal{T}+1]
.
Hence,
T_{0}=\mathcal{O}(\mathcal{T})=\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}{\delta})

and by choosing
n^{\prime}=\Theta(\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\wedge(\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}})^{2})

\varphi^{T_{0}}>\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}}=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)

The proof is complete.

∎

E.5
.

(Proposition
5.5
in Section
5
: Convergence of Angle)
In the noiseless setting, suppose
\varphi^{0}\geq\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)
, run Easy finite-sample EM for
T_{1}=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\right)
iterations followed by the standard finite-sample EM for at most
T^{\prime}=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{n}{d}\wedge\log\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\right)

iterations with all the same
n=\Omega\left(d\vee\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)
samples, then it satisfies

\varphi^{T}\geq\frac{\pi}{2}-\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right),

(19)

with probability at least
1-T\delta
, where
T:=T_{1}+T^{\prime},\varphi^{0}:=\frac{\pi}{2}-\arccos\left|\frac{\langle\theta^{0},\theta^{\ast}\rangle}{\|\theta^{0}\|\cdot\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\right|
and
\varphi^{T}:=\frac{\pi}{2}-\arccos\left|\frac{\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle}{\|\theta^{T}\|\cdot\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\right|
.

Proof.

In this proof, we assume that
\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)\leq\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)<0.1
, and denote by
\Theta:=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)
the threshold for
\varphi
.

Besides, we denote
\bar{\theta},\bar{\varphi}
for the EM update at population level.

We divide the whole procedure inito three stages.

In Stage 1,
\varphi\geq\operatorname{const}\cdot\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)\Rightarrow\varphi\geq\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)
after at most
T_{1}
iterations of Easy EM.

In Stage 2,
\varphi\geq 4\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)\Rightarrow\varphi>\arctan 1.5

after at most
T_{2}
iterations of standard EM.

In Stage 3,
\varphi>\arctan 1.5\Rightarrow\varphi^{t}>\frac{\pi}{2}-1.775\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)

after at most
T_{2}
iterations of standard EM.

Stage 1:
\varphi\geq\operatorname{const}\cdot\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)\Rightarrow\varphi\geq\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)

In the first Stage, we run Easy EM
\theta^{t+1}\leftarrow M_{n}^{\text{easy}}(\theta^{t},\nu^{t})
, and note that the length of the projected vector is less than or equal to the length of the original one

\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{t+1}-M(\theta^{t}),\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\leq\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\left\|\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}-\mathbb{E}_{\{x_{i}\}_{i\in[n]}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})}\right\}|\langle\tilde{x},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\mathrm{sgn}\langle\tilde{x},\theta^{t}\rangle\tilde{x}\right\|_{2}.

With probability at least
1-4\delta

\frac{1}{|\theta^{\ast}|}\left\|\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}-\mathbb{E}_{\{x_{i}\}_{i\in[n]}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})}\right\}|\langle\tilde{x},\theta^{\ast}\rangle|\mathrm{sgn}\langle\tilde{x},\theta^{t}\rangle\tilde{x}\right\|_{2}<\max\left(20\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}},45\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right):=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)

By using the assumption
\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)\leq\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)<0.1

\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{t+1}-M(\theta^{t}),\hat{e}_{1}\rangle<0.1

Then
\frac{\|\theta^{t+1}\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\leq 1+\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)<1.1

Use
\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{t+1},\vec{e}_{1}\rangle\right|\leq 2
, and with assumption
\varphi^{t}<\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)\leq 0.1

We denote
\Theta:=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)
the threshold for
\varphi
.

\displaystyle\sin\varphi^{t+1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\|\theta^{t+1}\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\right)^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|^{2}}\langle\theta^{t+1},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\right|=\left(\frac{\|\theta^{t+1}\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\right)^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{t+1},\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1.1^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{t+1},\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1.1^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{t+1}-M(\theta^{t}),\hat{e}_{1}\rangle+\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle M(\theta^{t}),\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1.1^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle M(\theta^{t}),\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\right|-1.1^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{t+1}-M(\theta^{t}),\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1.1^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle M(\theta^{t}),\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\right|-1.1^{-1}\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1.1^{-1}\cdot 1.239\cdot\left|\frac{1}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\langle\theta^{t},\hat{e}_{1}\rangle\right|-1.1^{-1}\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1.1263\cdot\sin\varphi^{t}-1.1^{-1}\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)

That is

Number of Iterations, Statistical Error

We denote
\Theta:=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)<0.1
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{1}{\Theta}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{1}{\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}}\wedge\log\frac{1}{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\wedge\log\frac{1}{\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{n}{d}\wedge\log\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\right)

In Stage 1, it taks at most

.

In Stage 2, it taks at most

.

In Stage 3, it taks at most

.

Hence, the iteration numbers at most to ensure the convergence
\varphi^{T}\geq\frac{\pi}{2}-\Theta
with probability at least
1-T\delta
is

\displaystyle T=T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\right)

For a good initialization
\varphi^{0}\geq 4\Theta

T^{\prime}=T_{2}+T_{3}=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{n}{d}\wedge\log\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\right)

∎

E.3
Error of Mixing Weights
\pi
and Convergence at the Finite-Sample Level

E.6
.

Let
q:=\max(p,1-p)
for
V_{i}\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\operatorname{Bern}(p),\forall i\in[n]
, then

for
t\in\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}])\geq t\right)\leq\exp(-2nt^{2})

for
t\in[\mathrm{e}(1-q),q)\neq\varnothing

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}])\geq t\right)\leq\exp\left\{-n\left\{\frac{t}{q}\left[\log\frac{t}{(1-q)}-1\right]+\frac{t^{2}}{2q^{2}}\right\}\right\}

for
t\in[q,\infty)

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}])\geq t\right)=0

Proof.

Let’s denote
q:=\max(p,1-p),V^{\prime}:=V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}],i\in[n]
, thus
\mathbb{E}\left[{V^{\prime}}^{2}\right]=\operatorname{Var}[V_{i}]=p(1-p)=q(1-q),|V^{\prime}|\leq q
.With Chenorff bound and let
\psi(\lambda):=\mathbb{E}[\exp(\lambda(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}]))]=-\lambda p+\log(1+p(\exp(\lambda)-1))\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}}{8}

\displaystyle\log\mathbb{P}[\sum_{i\in[n]}(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}])\geq nt]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\inf_{\lambda>0}\left\{\log\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\lambda\sum_{i\in[n]}(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}])\right)\right]-\lambda nt\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\inf_{\lambda>0}\left\{n\left[\frac{\lambda^{2}}{8}-\lambda t\right]\right\}=-2nt^{2}

Hence, the first probability inequality is proved.

Let’s focus on next concentration inequality. We begin with bounding
\psi(\lambda)
, note that
2\sinh(x)>\exp(x)-x-1,\forall x>0
.

\displaystyle\psi(\lambda)=1+\sum_{k\geq 2}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[{V^{\prime}}^{k}\right]}{k!}\lambda^{k}\leq 1+\sum_{k\geq 2}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[{V^{\prime}}^{2}\right]\cdot q^{k-2}}{k!}\lambda^{k}\leq 1+\frac{(1-q)}{q}\{\exp(q\lambda)-q\lambda-1\}\leq 1+2\frac{(1-q)}{q}\sinh(q\lambda)

Let
\mu:=2\frac{(1-q)}{q}\sinh(q\lambda)
, then
\lambda=\frac{1}{q}\operatorname{arcsinh}\left(\frac{q}{2(1-q)}\mu\right)
,
and
\mu^{\prime}:=\frac{q}{(1-q)}\mu
,
\gamma:=\frac{(1-q)}{q}\in(0,1],\tau:=\frac{t}{(1-q)}
.

\displaystyle\log\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}])\geq t\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\inf_{\lambda>0}\sum_{i\in[n]}\log\mathbb{E}[\exp(\lambda(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}]))]-\log\exp(n\lambda t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\inf_{\lambda>0}[\log\psi(\lambda)-\lambda t]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\inf_{\lambda>0}\left\{\log\left[1+2\frac{(1-q)}{q}\sinh(q\lambda)\right]-\lambda t\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\gamma\inf_{\mu^{\prime}>0}\left\{\frac{1}{\gamma}\log(1+\gamma\mu^{\prime})-\tau\operatorname{arcsinh}\left(\frac{\mu^{\prime}}{2}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\gamma\inf_{\mu^{\prime}>0}\left\{\frac{1}{\gamma}\log(1+\gamma\mu^{\prime})-\tau\log(\mu^{\prime})\right\}

If
t\geq q
, then
\tau:=\frac{t}{(1-q)}\in[\frac{1}{\gamma},\infty)
.

n\gamma\inf_{\mu^{\prime}>0}\left\{\frac{1}{\gamma}\log(1+\gamma\mu^{\prime})-\tau\log(\mu^{\prime})\right\}=-\infty

If
t\in[\mathrm{e}(1-q),q)
, then
\tau:=\frac{t}{(1-q)}\in[\mathrm{e},\frac{1}{\gamma})
.

\displaystyle n\gamma\inf_{\mu^{\prime}>0}\left\{\frac{1}{\gamma}\log(1+\gamma\mu^{\prime})-\tau\log(\mu^{\prime})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\gamma\left\{\frac{1}{\gamma}\log(1+\gamma\mu^{\prime})-\tau\log(\mu^{\prime})\right\}_{\mu^{\prime}=\frac{\tau}{1-\gamma\tau}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\gamma\left\{-\tau\log\tau+\frac{1}{\gamma}[-(1-\gamma\tau)\log(1-\gamma\tau)]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\gamma\left\{-\tau\log\tau+\frac{\gamma\tau}{\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma\tau}{2}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\gamma\left\{-\tau[\log\tau-1]-\frac{\gamma}{2}\tau^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\left\{-\frac{t}{q}\left[\log\frac{t}{(1-q)}-1\right]-\frac{t^{2}}{2q^{2}}\right\}

Therefore, the third probability inequality is proved, and we show the probability is 0 when
t\geq q
.

∎

E.7
.

(Theorem
5.1
in Section
5
: Convergence at the Finite-Sample Level)
In the noiseless setting, suppose any initial mixing weights
\pi^{0}
and any initial regression parameters
\theta^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
ensuring that
\varphi^{0}\geq\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right)
. If we
run finite-sample Easy EM for at most
T_{1}=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\right)
iterations followed by the finite-sample standard EM for at most
T^{\prime}=\mathcal{O}\left(\log\frac{n}{d}\wedge\log\frac{n}{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}\right)

iterations with all the same
n=\Omega\left(d\vee\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)
samples, then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\|\theta^{T+1}-\mathrm{sgn}(\rho^{T+1})\theta^{\ast}\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right),

\displaystyle\|\pi^{T+1}-\bar{\pi}^{\ast}\|_{1}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{2}-\pi^{\ast}\right\|_{1}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+c(\pi^{\ast})\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right),

with probability at least
1-T\delta
, where
T:=T_{1}+T^{\prime},\varphi^{0}:=\frac{\pi}{2}-\arccos\left|\frac{\langle\theta^{0},\theta^{\ast}\rangle}{\|\theta^{0}\|\cdot\|\theta^{\ast}\|}\right|,\rho^{T+1}:=\frac{\langle\theta^{T+1},\theta^{\ast}\rangle}{\|\theta^{T+1}\|\cdot\|\theta^{\ast}\|},\bar{\pi}^{\ast}:=\frac{1}{2}-\operatorname{sgn}(\rho^{0})(\frac{1}{2}-\pi^{\ast})
, and the
coefficient
c(\pi^{\ast})=\mathcal{O}(1)
, especially
c(\pi^{\ast})=0
when
\pi^{\ast}=\{1,0\}
or
\{0,1\}
.

Proof.

In the proof of Proposition
5.5
for the converge of angle
\varphi
, we show that EM upate rules ensure
\varphi^{T}>\frac{\pi}{2}-1.775\Theta
after enough
T
iterations, where
\Theta:=\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)
is the threshold for
\varphi
.

Using the relation
\phi^{T}:=2\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\varphi^{T}\right)\in(0,\pi)
, namely

\phi^{T}<3.55\Theta

In the following proof, we use
\bar{\nu}^{T+1}
for the EM update at the population level.

Final Statistical Error in Regression Parameters
\theta

Note that
\sqrt{(\phi-\sin\phi)^{2}+(1-\cos\phi)^{2}}\leq\frac{\phi^{2}}{2}

for
\forall\phi\in\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]
, and use tha assumption
\Theta<0.1
in Proposition
5.5
, the upper bound for
the relative error is

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|M_{n}(\theta^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle{\theta^{\ast}}\right\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|M(\theta^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle{\theta^{\ast}}\right\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}+\frac{\|M_{n}(\theta^{T})-M(\theta^{T})\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi}\sqrt{(\phi^{T}-\sin\phi^{T})^{2}+(1-\cos\phi^{T})^{2}}+\Theta

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{[\phi^{T}]^{2}}{2\pi}+\Theta

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\frac{3.55^{2}}{2\pi}\Theta^{2}+\Theta

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\left(\frac{3.55^{2}}{2\pi}\cdot 0.1+1\right)\Theta

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle 1.21\Theta

Hence,
\frac{\|\theta^{T+1}-\mathrm{sgn}(\rho^{T+1})\theta^{\ast}\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}=\frac{\left\|M_{n}(\theta^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle{\theta^{\ast}}\right\|}{\|\theta^{\ast}\|}=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)

Final Statistical Error in Mixing Weights
\pi

By using the Corollary
3.3
in Section
3
, and note that
\tanh(\bar{\nu}^{T+1}):=N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})
.

\displaystyle\tanh(\bar{\nu}^{T+1})=N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})=\tanh(\nu^{\ast})\cdot\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\left[\frac{2}{\pi}\varphi^{T}\right]

Note that
\mathbb{E}\left[N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})\right]=N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})=\tanh(\bar{\nu}^{T+1})
, in the noiseless setting, Lemma in Appendix
C
gives that.

\displaystyle\tanh(\nu^{t+1})=N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{T}\rangle\cdot(-1)^{z_{i}+1}

Note that
x_{i},z_{i}
are independent, let
W_{i}:=\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{T}\rangle\cdot(-1)^{z_{i}+1}
.

with
\mathbb{P}(z_{i}=1)=\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\tanh(\nu^{\ast}),\mathbb{P}(z_{i}=2)=\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\tanh(\nu^{\ast})

Using Lemma
C.8
(Grothendieck’s Identity), we show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{T}\rangle=+1]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\frac{1}{2}+\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\frac{\varphi^{T}}{\pi}\right]

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{T}\rangle=-1]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\frac{1}{2}-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\frac{\varphi^{T}}{\pi}\right]

Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[W_{i}=+1]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{T}\rangle=+1]\cdot\mathbb{P}(z_{i}=1)+\mathbb{P}[\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\mathrm{sgn}\langle x_{i},\theta^{T}\rangle=-1]\cdot\mathbb{P}(z_{i}=2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}+\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\frac{\varphi^{T}}{\pi}\cdot\tanh(\nu^{\ast})

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[W_{i}=-1]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\frac{\varphi^{T}}{\pi}\cdot\tanh(\nu^{\ast})

Let
V_{i}:=\frac{1}{2}(W_{i}+1)\overset{\operatorname{iid}}{\sim}\operatorname{Bern}(p)
be Bernoulli distribution with the parameter
p:=\frac{1}{2}+\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\frac{\varphi^{T}}{\pi}\cdot\tanh(\nu^{\ast})
.

Therefore, with
\frac{1}{2}N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})+\frac{1}{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}V_{i}
and
\frac{1}{2}N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})+\frac{1}{2}=\mathbb{E}[V_{i}]=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}\mathbb{E}[V_{i}]
.

By using the probability inequalities in Lemma
E.6

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[|N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})|\geq 2t]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}])\right|\geq t\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp(-2nt^{2})

Consequently, by letting
\exp(-2nt^{2})\leftarrow\delta
, with
probability at least
1-2\delta

|N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})|<\sqrt{\frac{2\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}

Furthermore, when
\pi^{\ast}=[1,0]
or
[0,1]
, namely
\nu^{\ast}\to\pm\infty
,
then
q:=\max(p,1-p)\to\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\varphi^{T}}{\pi}
.
Since after enough iterations, EM updates ensure that
\varphi^{T}>\frac{\pi}{2}-1.775
.
Hence,
q:=\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\varphi^{T}}{\pi}>1-0.565\Theta,1-q<0.565\Theta
.

Using Lemma
E.6
, we show that for
t\in[\mathrm{e}(1-q),q)\neq\varnothing

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}(V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}])\geq t\right)\leq\exp\left\{-n\left\{\frac{t}{q}\left[\log\frac{t}{(1-q)}-1\right]+\frac{t^{2}}{2q^{2}}\right\}\right\}

By choosing
t=0.565\mathrm{e}\Theta\in[\mathrm{e}(1-q),\infty)
, note that
\Theta^{2}\geq\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}
, then with probability at least

\displaystyle 1-\exp\left\{-n\left\{\frac{t}{q}\left[\log\frac{t}{(1-q)}-1\right]+\frac{t^{2}}{2q^{2}}\right\}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\exp\left\{-n\frac{0.565^{2}\mathrm{e}^{2}}{2}\Theta^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\exp\left\{-1.179n\cdot\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\delta

Hence
|N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})|=2\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}|V_{i}-\mathbb{E}[V_{i}]|\geq 2\cdot 0.565\mathrm{e}\Theta=1.13\mathrm{e}\Theta
with probability at least
1-2\delta
, when
\pi^{\ast}=[1,0]
or
[0,1]
.

\displaystyle|N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|+|N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|1-\frac{2}{\pi}\varphi^{T}\right|\cdot|\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|+\sqrt{\frac{2\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle 1.775\frac{2}{\pi}\Theta\cdot|\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|+\sqrt{\frac{2\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1.13\Theta\cdot|\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|+\sqrt{\frac{2\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1.13|\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)+\sqrt{\frac{2\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}

In the proof, we use
|N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|=\left|1-\frac{2}{\pi}\varphi^{T}\right|\cdot|\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|
, which is provided in Corollary
4.2
.

Particularly, when
\pi^{\ast}=\{1,0\}
or
\{0,1\}
, then
|\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|=1

\displaystyle|N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|+|N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-N(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})|

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle 1.13\Theta\cdot|\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|+1.13\mathrm{e}\Theta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1.13(1+\mathrm{e})|\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|\Theta\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)

Therefore
\|\pi^{T+1}-\bar{\pi}^{\ast}\|_{1}=|N_{n}(\theta^{T},\nu^{T})-\operatorname{sgn}\langle\theta^{T},\theta^{\ast}\rangle\tanh(\nu^{\ast})|=\left\|\frac{1}{2}-\pi^{\ast}\right\|_{1}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d}{n}}\vee\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)+c(\pi^{\ast})\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\delta}}{n}}\right)
, where
c(\pi^{\ast})=0
when
\pi^{\ast}=\{1,0\}
or
\{0,1\}
, and
c(\pi^{\ast})=\mathcal{O}(1)
.

∎
